src/gpu/zn_mlp.zig

declarationwhat it is
configFloats per buffer: 256k (1 MiB).
ActWhat a layer's output goes through.
ParamsEverything a dispatch needs, written by the host before it.
acts_lenThe activation buffer is the one that grows: eight times the others.
Buffers—
g—
dense_fwdy[r, o] = act(b[o] + sum_i x[r, i] W[i, o]); one thread per (r, o).
act_bwddy[r, o] *= act'(y[r, o]), in place: the gradient at the pre-activation.
dense_bwd_xdx[r, i] = sum_o dz[r, o] W[i, o]; one thread per (r, i).
dense_bwd_wgW[i, o] = sum_r x[r, i] dz[r, o], then gb[o] = sum_r dz[r, o]: one thread per weight.
mse_bwddy = 2 (y - t) / n over the rows x out_dim outputs: the gradient of their mean square error.
mse_valueloss[0] = mean (y - t)^2, one thread: for the readback, not for training.
ppo_mean_gradd loss / d mean[r, k]: one thread per (row, action dimension), into dacts[dy_off].
ppo_logstd_gradd loss / d log_std[k]: one thread per action dimension, summed over the rows, into grads.
adamAdam over count elements from w_off, with the host's bias corrections.
squash_fwdu = mean + std eps, a = tanh(u): u kept at acts[u_off], the action written into a strided block (acts[z_off + r stride + col + k]).
squash_logplog pi per row: sum over dimensions of -eps^2/2 - log std - log(2 pi)/2 - log(1 - tanh(u)^2).
sac_targety = r + gamma (1 - done)(min(q1', q2') - alpha log pi'): q1' at acts[y_off], q2' at acts[z_off], log pi' at acts[l_off], rewards then dones at acts[x_off]; y to acts[t_off].
min_routeThe actor loss's -mean(min(q1, q2)) gradient, routed WHOLE to the smaller critic (ties go to the first, as zimrnum's graph min): into dacts[dy_off] for q1 and dacts[dx_off] for q2.
squash_bwddL/du for the actor loss mean(alpha log pi - min q): the critics' gradient at their action input (both blocks, dacts[dx_off] and dacts[e_off], strided; one of each row's is zero) through tanh, plus alpha's pull through the squash correction (d/du of -log(1 - tanh^2) is 2 tanh).
squash_logstd_gradThe raw log-std's gradient: sum over rows of dL/du std eps, less alpha (the -log std in log pi, averaged), through the tanh bound.
alpha_gradLog alpha's gradient, -alpha (mean log pi + target entropy): zn.Temperature.gradient.
polyaktarget = tau online + (1 - tau) target, over count params: target at w_off, online at v_off.
cp_wm_input[cart rate, pole angle, pole rate] of the state at acts[x_off] and the force at acts[u_off] into the world model's input rows at acts[z_off].
cp_wm_input_bwdThe world-model input's gradient (dacts[dx_off], rows x 4) back to the state: ADDED into the state gradient at dacts[dy_off] (columns 1-3); the force's share to dacts[e_off].
cp_forceThe policy's force: tanh(o + sigma eps), o at acts[y_off], eps at acts[x_off], into acts[u_off].
cp_force_bwdd loss / d o: the force's gradient (dacts[e_off]) through tanh, plus the action penalty's 2 w_action o / rows.
cp_stepOne integration step: the state at acts[x_off], the world model's output at acts[y_off] (rows x 2), the next state to acts[z_off].
cp_step_bwdcp_step's adjoint: the next state's gradient (dacts[dy_off]) to the state's (dacts[e_off], OVERWRITTEN - this starts the step's state gradient) and the world model output's (dacts[dx_off], rows x 2).
cp_track_bwdThe tracking loss's gradient at a state (acts[x_off], rows x 4) against a target (acts[t_off]): 2 w_c (s - t) / rows, into dacts[dy_off] - ADDED when accumulate.
add_blockdacts[dy_off + i] += dacts[dx_off + i] over count: a gradient arriving by a second path.
lat_advanceThe next state, in two places at once: z_{k+1} = z_k + change, written into the next block's z columns (strided, so the next step's first layer reads it) AND into a contiguous block (the loss kernels read contiguous rows).
lat_takeThe backward join: the gradient that reached a block's z columns (strided, at dx_off) added into the state's contiguous gradient (at dy_off) - the path by which the loss at later steps reaches earlier states THROUGH the network, beside the residual path add_block carries.
lat_gatherA batch of training windows, assembled on the GPU from what is resident there: the feature ring (a record per character per step: raw features, action, table row), the reference tables (a row per clip frame: raw goal features, encoded targets) and the normaliser (means, then spreads).
latRecordWidthFloats in one record of the feature ring: a character's raw (unnormalised) features, the action it took on that step, and one more for which reference-table row it was aiming at.
latRecordActionWhere a record's action begins.
latRecordTableRowWhere a record's reference-table row sits: the last column, kept as a float like everything else in the buffer and rounded back to an integer when read.
latTableWidthFloats in one row of the reference tables: a frame's features, then the servo targets the fleet would aim at for it - everything a rollout needs about a reference frame, so a window can be built without the clips being on the device at all.
latNoise—
latStepSeedA different seed for every step of the rollout, from one seed per training step.
lat_actThe policy's action into its row: lat_scale * raw into the wide row's action columns, so the world model's next step reads the action the policy just chose.
lat_act_bwdThe action's gradient back to the policy's raw output: d raw = lat_scale * d action + lat_penalty * raw.
st_policy_fwdThe policy's window forward, one thread per row: starts at acts[z_off], noise per step at acts[y_off + step rows + r].
st_world_fwdThe world model's window forward, one thread per row: the real start at acts[z_off], the real forces per step at acts[y_off + step rows + r].
st_policy_bwdThe policy's window backward, one thread per row, in reverse: the tracking gradient (target upright, zeros), the step's adjoint, back through the world model to its input (no world-model gradients), the force, the policy - its weight gradients into this row's slice.
st_world_bwdThe world model's window backward, one thread per row, in reverse: the gradient against the real next states (acts[t_off + (step rows + r) 4]), the step's adjoint, the world model's backward WITH its weight gradients into this row's slice.
st_reducegrads[w_off + j] = sum over rows of the per-row gradients (dacts[dx_off + r count + j]).
kernels—
